Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Reconstructing articulated 3D objects is important for animation, gaming, and robotic simulations. Recent neural networks can estimate the articulated structure of 3D objects, but their generalization remains limited by the scarcity of annotated data for this task. To address this gap, we introduce Instruct-Particulate, a model that takes a 3D mesh together with a target kinematic specification, including part descriptions, connectivity, joint types, and optional point prompts, and predicts the corresponding kinematic part segmentation and joint motion parameters. The kinematic specification disambiguates the task and allows the model to target annotations of different granularity, thereby making it possible to use more abundant heterogeneous training data. At test time, the kinematic specification can be obtained automatically from large-scale vision-language models, so the model can be applied to any input mesh. To train our model at scale, we construct a heterogeneous dataset of more than 150,000 articulated 3D objects, extending existing publicly available collections with data obtained by partially labelling other 3D models (monolithic or already decomposed into parts) with kinematic labels by means of vision-language models. Experiments show that our model generalizes better across categories and to AI-generated meshes, enabling articulated asset reconstruction from real-world images via image-to-3D models.

</details>


### [57] [S$^2$COPE: Self-Supervised Concept Discovery via Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14586)
*Shilong Xiang,Zirui Zhang,Chengzhi Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCOPE是一种无标签的自监督框架，它利用视觉语言大模型在偏好学习中自主发现可解释的概念，从而解决表示学习中可扩展性和可解释性之间的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前表示学习存在一个根本矛盾：自监督方法可扩展到大规模数据集但生成的特征不透明、难以解释；而可解释的模型则受限于需要大量密集的人工标注。本文旨在解决这一困境，实现既可扩展又可解释的表示学习。

Method: 提出SCOPE框架，一个无标签的解决方案。它不将视觉语言大模型视为静态的特征提取器，而是将其作为自监督偏好优化循环中的主动参与者。通过自主地对原始图像中的候选视觉属性进行假设、验证和强化，从而在没有标注的情况下发现新的、结构化的概念。该方法通过自监督偏好目标将概念发现直接整合到VLLM骨干网络中，而非依赖静态生成和分离过滤。

Result: 在自然、医学和物理领域的广泛实验表明，SCOPE成功提取了领域特定的概念，而标准的VLLM往往无法生成这些概念。通过将概念发现直接整合到VLLM骨干网络中，在未见数据的下游top-1分类准确率上实现了高达24个百分点的绝对提升。

Conclusion: 可解释性可以通过模型与偶然视觉结构的自主交互来涌现，而无需任何人类监督。SCOPE框架为解决可扩展性与可解释性之间的矛盾提供了一条有效路径。

Abstract: Current representation learning paradigms force a fundamental compromise: self-supervised methods scale to massive datasets but yield opaque features, whereas interpretable models remain bottlenecked by the need for dense human annotation. We introduce Self-Supervised Concept discOvery via Preference lEarning (\model), a label-free framework that resolves this dilemma. Instead of treating Vision-Large-Language Models (VLLMs) as static feature extractors, \model leverages them as active participants in a self-supervised preference optimization loop. By autonomously hypothesizing, validating, and reinforcing candidate visual attributes directly from raw imagery, our framework discovers novel, structured concepts without a single label. Extensive experiments across natural, medical, and physics domains demonstrate that \model successfully extracts domain-specific concepts where standard VLLMs often fail to generate. By amortizing concept discovery directly into the VLLM backbone through our self-supervised preference objective -- rather than relying on static generation and disjoint filtering -- we achieve up to a 24-point absolute improvement in downstream top-1 classification accuracy on unseen data. Our work suggest that interpretability can emerge through a model's autonomous interaction with incidental visual structures, without any human supervision.

</details>


### [58] [StereoGeo: an end-to-end stereo camera calibration method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14619)
*Imane Meddour,Andréa Macario Barros,Cédric Gouy-Pailler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StereoGeo是一种用于立体相机标定的端到端网络方法，可同时估计左右相机的内参（焦距、重力方向）和相对外参，性能优于现有单目方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机标定方法存在局限：依赖标定图案、仅处理单相机配置、局限于内在或外部估计、需要多视图设置。StereoGeo旨在扩展GeoCalib算法，实现端到端的立体相机内外参联合估计。

Method: 扩展GeoCalib算法，结合深度神经网络特征提取与可微优化器，构建端到端的立体标定网络。通过神经网络提取特征，利用可微优化器联合优化焦距、重力方向和相机间的相对外参变换。

Result: 在真实世界基准数据集上的实验表明，StereoGeo在内在标定方面达到了有竞争力的性能，并提供了准确的立体外部估计。该方法优于仅限于单目设置的现有方法。数据集部分已公开。

Conclusion: StereoGeo成功实现了端到端的立体相机标定，克服了传统方法的局限性，在真实场景中表现出优越的性能。该工作推动了深度学习在相机标定领域的应用，并提供了公开数据集支持后续研究。

Abstract: In this work, we propose StereoGeo, an end-to-end network-based approach for stereo camera calibration. Our method estimates the focal lengths and gravity directions of the left and right cameras, as well as the relative extrinsic transformation relating them. Existing methods often rely on calibration patterns in structured environments or address only a single camera configuration, being limited to either intrinsic or extrinsic estimation, and depending on a multi-view setups. StereoGeo extends the GeoCalib algorithm, integrating deep neural network feature extraction with a differentiable optimizer. Extensive experiments on real-world benchmarks demonstrate that StereoGeo achieves competitive performance for intrinsic calibration and provides accurate stereo extrinsic estimation, outperforming existing methods that are limited to monocular settings. The dataset used in this work is partially publicly available at https://github.com/meddourimane/StereoGeo-dataset.

</details>


### [59] [SED:Lightweight Saliency prediction for Event-based data via Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14631)
*Romaric Mazna,Jean Martinet,Michele Magno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于事件相机显著性预测的轻量级深度神经网络，该网络通过知识蒸馏训练，基于一种名为深度时空模块（DSTconv）的创新结构。该模型在大幅缩减模型尺寸和参数量的同时，保持了优异的性能，并展现出卓越的跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文的主要动机是推动事件相机在边缘计算中的高效应用。事件相机能输出高时间分辨率的事件流，将其与显著性预测相结合，可为下游感知任务提供高效的前端处理。然而，现有方法要么性能不足，要么计算成本过高。作者旨在设计一种能有效利用事件数据时间信息，同时保持模型轻量化的解决方案。

Method: 作者提出了一个基于“深度时空模块（DSTconv）”的轻量级网络架构。该模块是对3D深度可分离卷积进行因式分解得到的，旨在高效地提取事件数据中的时空特征。整个模型采用知识蒸馏的方式进行训练，即利用一个大型、高性能的“教师模型”来指导这个轻量级“学生模型”的学习，从而在压缩模型的同时尽可能保留性能。

Result: 1. 模型压缩效果显著：相较于教师模型，所提模型的尺寸从180MB降至0.32MB（压缩562倍），参数量从45M降至81K（减少554倍）。
2. 性能优越：在N-DHF1K和N-UCF Sports两个事件相机显著性数据集上，该轻量级模型的性能达到或超过了其大型教师模型。
3. 泛化能力强：模型展现出强大的跨域泛化能力。在从合成事件数据迁移到真实事件数据的测试中，该模型表现良好，而一个从头开始训练的模型则失败了。

Conclusion: 该研究成功设计了一种用于事件相机显著性预测的高效轻量级网络。通过创新的DSTconv模块和知识蒸馏训练策略，该模型在保持极低计算和存储开销的同时，实现了与大型模型相当甚至更优的性能，并具备优异的跨域泛化能力。这为事件相机视觉在资源受限的边缘计算场景中的实际应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: Event-based saliency prediction has gained attention recently, as combining event cameras with saliency estimation can act as an upstream stage that naturally improves the efficiency of downstream eventbased perception at the edge. However, current approaches are either neuromorphic, underperforming on event-based saliency benchmarks, or too heavy for resource-constrained edge applications due to their reliance on transformers or 3D convolutions. Drawing inspiration from efficient convolutional modules, SED and aiming to exploit the temporal information in event data, we propose a lightweight network, trained through knowledge distillation, built on a Depthwise Spatio-Temporal Block (DSTconv) -- a factorization of the 3D depthwise separable convolution. Relative to its teacher, our model reduces the model size from 180 MB to 0.32 MB (562x) and the parameter count from 45M to 81k (554x), while matching or outperforming it on the N-DHF1K and N-UCF Sports datasets. Moreover, it generalizes strongly beyond its training distribution, transferring from synthetic to real event data where a model trained from scratch fails.

</details>


### [60] [Improving Lunar Topography with Deep Learning Schrödinger Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14638)
*Matthew Repasky,Erwan Mazarico,Michael K. Barker,Stefano Bertone,Terence J. Sabaka,Yao Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于扩散的Schrödinger Bridge生成模型，用于月球地形超分辨率重建，结合物理约束的光学图像，在保证大规模应用可行性的同时提供像素级不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高行星地形模型分辨率对理解表面过程和地貌至关重要，但现有解析超分辨率方法计算成本高、难以大规模应用。生成模型能利用硬件加速学习数据复杂关系并支持并行化，为大规模地形超分辨率提供了新途径。

Method: 采用基于扩散的Schrödinger Bridge生成模型，建立低分辨率与高分辨率地形分布之间的映射，并融入具有物理约束的光学图像信息。该方法受Shape-from-Shading技术启发，利用目标分辨率的光学图像优化先验低分辨率地形。模型在模拟月球勘测轨道器窄角相机光学图像的月球地形渲染数据集上进行训练。

Result: 提出了一种灵活的地形超分辨率方法，能够在重建过程中提供像素级的不确定性估计，为大规模行星地形建模提供了可扩展的生成式解决方案。

Conclusion: 本研究验证了生成模型在行星地形超分辨率任务中的可行性和优势，所提方法不仅具有计算可扩展性，还能提供不确定性量化，为未来高分辨率地形建模和地貌分析提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Increasing the resolution of planetary topography models can enable a better understanding of surface processes and geomorphology; however, existing analytical super-resolution methods are expensive and difficult to apply at large scales. Generative models provide the tools to learn complex relationships within data and can be applied at scale due to hardware accelerators and parallelization. We present a diffusion-based Schrödinger Bridge (SB) generative modeling approach for lunar topography super-resolution, connecting the distribution of low-resolution topography to that of high-resolution topography, incorporating physically-constraining optical imagery. Our approach is inspired by existing Shape-from-Shading methods, which improve a priori low-resolution topography by using optical images at the target resolution. We train SBs on a novel dataset of rendered lunar topography, emulating optical imagery from the Lunar Reconnaissance Orbiter Narrow Angle Camera. The result is a flexible approach for topography super-resolution which can provide pixel-level uncertainties in the reconstruction.

</details>


### [61] [HPSv3++: Scaling Reward Models Across the Full Spectrum of Diffusion Model Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14657)
*Yijun Liu,Jie Huang,Zeyue Xue,Yuming Li,Ruizhe He,Haoran Li,Shijia Ge,Siming Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HPSv3++是一个改进的奖励模型框架，通过引入新的双维度偏好数据集HPDv3++和两阶段训练方法，使奖励模型能够适应不同文本到图像（T2I）模型的能力和强化学习迭代变化，在偏好预测和RL训练中表现优于前代模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的奖励模型（如HPSv3）是基于早期T2I模型的预标注数据训练的，没有考虑到T2I模型能力持续提升和强化学习迭代过程中可能带来的质量判别标准变化（即能力-迭代谱系偏移），这限制了它们在新兴模型和迭代中的适用性和指导效果。

Method: 1. 数据集构建：创建了HPDv3++数据集，包含21.2万对针对文本保真度和美学质量两个维度的偏好数据，由近期高能力模型Qwen-Image在人工监督下进行标注。2. 两阶段训练：第一阶段采用数据感知的正交梯度投影，在从HPDv3++中学习多样化美学感知的同时，保留HPSv3中有效的原始人类偏好知识。第二阶段利用来自不同能力水平和RL迭代阶段的T2I模型的未标注数据，引入联合能力-迭代条件信号，以及基于标准差的无监督引导机制，从而增强模型在整个能力-迭代谱系上的泛化能力。

Result: HPSv3++在偏好预测任务上达到先进水平：在HPDv3基准上比HPSv3提升9.8%，在GenAI-Bench上提升5.5%，并在新提出的HPDv3++基准上达到79.1%和88.1%的准确率。将其用于T2I模型的强化学习（RL）训练时，能在多种不同的T2I模型上持续提升GenEval分数，证明了其广泛的适用性和有效性。

Conclusion: HPSv3++通过创新的数据集构建和两阶段训练框架，有效解决了奖励模型在动态发展的T2I领域中适应模型能力变化和RL迭代更新的挑战。该方法显著提升了奖励模型的泛化能力和指导效果，为构建更鲁棒、更通用的T2I对齐奖励模型提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reward models guide text-to-image (T2I) systems toward outputs aligned with human preferences. However, typical reward models such as HPSv3 are trained on pre-annotated data from earlier T2I models, without accounting for quality discriminative shifts arising from evolving model capabilities and reinforcement learning (RL) iterations, limiting their broader applicability. In this work, we propose HPSv3++, a reward model framework that elevates the HPSv3 model for varying T2I model capabilities and their RL iteration changes across the full capability-iteration spectrum. Specifically, we first introduce HPDv3++, a 212K dual-dimension preference dataset annotated for text fidelity and aesthetic quality using a recent high-capability (Qwen-Image) model with human supervision. We then propose a two-stage training framework. Stage 1 employs data-aware orthogonal gradient projection to incorporate diverse aesthetic perception from HPDv3++ while preserving the original effective human preference knowledge in HPSv3. Stage 2 further leverages unlabeled data from T2I models spanning different capability levels and RL iterations, and introduces a joint capability-iterations conditioned signal for the reward model together with a standard deviation-driven unsupervised guidance mechanism, strengthening reward model across the capability-iteration spectrum. HPSv3++ achieves state-of-the-art preference prediction, outperforming HPSv3 9.8% on HPDv3, 5.5% on GenAI-Bench, while achieving 79.1%/88.1% on our proposed HPDv3++. When used for T2I RL training, it consistently improves GenEval scores across diverse T2I models, demonstrating its wide-range capabilities. The code is available at https://github.com/PlantPotatoOnMoon/HPSv3-PlusPlus.

</details>


### [62] [Giving AI a Headache: Acoustic Adversarial Attacks to Computer Vision Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14658)
*Nicole Villavicencio-Garduño,Maksim Ekin Eren,Milo Prisbrey,Ben Migliori,Michael Teti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用可听频率（<20 kHz）声学振动攻击商用摄像头，以干扰其内部稳定机制并导致计算机视觉AI模型（如YOLO11）出错的可行性，旨在克服超声波攻击的距离限制并深入分析攻击影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于超声波（>20 kHz）的声学攻击由于高频衰减而局限于短距离应用。为了探索更实用、影响范围更广的攻击方式，本研究旨在调查使用可听频率（<20 kHz）进行声学攻击的可行性，并更全面地分析此类攻击对图像和物体特征的影响。

Method: 研究通过物理实验进行验证：使用各种可听频率的声波共振一个商用摄像头，旨在干扰其内部图像稳定系统。随后，在该受干扰的条件下，测试了一个现成的物体检测模型（YOLO11）的性能。此外，研究还扩展分析了攻击对不同图像和物体特征的影响。

Result: 实验成功演示了可听频率声学攻击的可行性，表明其能够有效干扰摄像头的稳定机制，并导致目标检测模型（YOLO11）出现误分类、漏检或目标幻觉等错误。研究结果揭示了若干使AI计算机视觉系统对此类攻击更脆弱的因素。

Conclusion: 可听频率声学攻击对广泛使用的AI计算机视觉系统构成真实且可行的威胁。本研究不仅验证了攻击的可行性，还通过分析关键影响因素，为未来开发针对性的防御和缓解策略提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) is increasingly used to automate a variety of real-world computer vision (CV) applications, such as autonomous vehicle control, facial recognition, and security cameras. Recent research has shown that acoustic vibration can induce real physical motion in cameras, interfering with their internal stabilization mechanisms. Because the motion falls outside the conditions the stabilization system was designed to handle, the system introduces artifacts into the frame, causing AI-based CV models to misclassify, miss targets, or hallucinate objects.
  Previous work used ultrasonic frequencies (>20 kHz) to perform short-range attacks, which limits them to short distances due to the attenuation exhibited by high frequencies. In this work, we investigate acoustic attacks using lower frequencies in the audible range (<20 kHz), and we further expand our analysis to include how various image and object features are affected by the attacks.
  Specifically, we performed physical experiments to demonstrate the viability of our attacks on an off-the-shelf object detection model (YOLO11) by resonating a commercially available camera with various frequencies. Based on our results, we provide insights into several factors that make an AI CV system more vulnerable to these attacks, which could help inform the development of future mitigation strategies.

</details>


### [63] [Memento: Reconstruct to Remember for Consistent Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14667)
*Xuan Wei,Longbin Ji,Guan Wang,Xiangrui Liu,Zhenyu Zhang,Shuohuan Wang,Yu Sun,Qingqi Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Memento框架，通过基于记忆的主题重建来解决长视频生成中主题一致性退化问题，显著提升了长期主题一致性、跨镜头连贯性和视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频生成方法虽采用分镜头生成以提高可扩展性，但主要关注生成合理的后续镜头，缺乏验证历史记忆是否保留了身份关键主题证据的机制，导致生成过程中主题特征可能被稀释、覆盖或遗忘。因此，需要将主题保持明确建模为身份定位问题。

Method: 提出Memento框架，其核心思想是：一个忠实保留主题的记忆库应能仅凭记忆支持该主题的重建。具体方法包括：1) 联合训练自回归下一镜头生成与基于记忆的主题重建（利用历史记忆和全局故事描述恢复目标外观）；2) 引入双查询记忆机制以解耦长程主题证据与短程线索（一个查询检索身份相关记忆，另一个查询选择短上下文关键帧以实现连贯续写）；3) 构建主题感知电影数据管道，通过一致的、无代词的主题描述提供精确重建监督。

Result: 实验表明，Memento在长期主题一致性、跨镜头连贯性和视觉质量方面均达到当前最优性能。

Conclusion: Memento通过显式建模主题保持为身份定位问题并引入基于记忆的重建指导，有效解决了长视频生成中的主题一致性退化难题，为长视频生成提供了一种可靠的主题一致性保障框架。

Abstract: Long-form video generation requires recurring subjects to remain consistent across various shots, viewpoints, motions, and scene transitions. Existing temporal decomposition methods improve scalability by generating videos shot by shot. However, they mainly focus on optimizing plausible next-shot continuations without verifying whether the historical memory preserves identity-critical subject evidence. Consequently, as generation proceeds, recurring subjects may be diluted, overwritten, or forgotten. In this paper, we propose Memento, a subject-reconstruction-guided framework that treats subject preservation as an explicit identity grounding problem, based on the premise that a memory bank faithfully preserving a subject should support reconstructing that subject from memory alone. Specifically, Memento jointly trains autoregressive next-shot generation with memory-based subject reconstruction, recovering target appearances using historical memory and global story captions. To disentangle long-range subject evidence from short-range cues, Memento introduces a dual-query memory mechanism, where one query retrieves identity-relevant memory and the other selects short-context keyframes for coherent continuation. Additionally, a subject-aware cinematic data pipeline provides precise reconstruction supervision via consistent, pronoun-free subject descriptions. Experiments demonstrate that Memento achieves state-of-the-art performance in long-term subject consistency, cross-shot coherence, and visual quality.

</details>


### [64] [HumP-KD: A Hybrid Uncertainty-Aware Multi-Stage Progressive Knowledge Distillation Framework for Efficient Fire Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14684)
*Mohammed Arif Mainuddin,Najifa Tabassum,Omar Ibne Shahid,Riasat Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为HumP-KD的混合不确定性感知多阶段渐进式知识蒸馏框架，用于高效火灾分类，可在资源受限硬件上部署的实时模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时火灾分类系统需要同时满足准确性、计算效率和可部署在资源受限硬件上的模型。

Method: 提出HumP-KD框架，通过层次渐进知识蒸馏和多阶段知识蒸馏，将两个异构transformer教师模型（Swin-Tiny和ViT-Base）及其集成的知识蒸馏到轻量级MobileViT-S学生模型中。

Result: 在Dataset-II上，HumP-KD取得0.9876 ± 0.0063的平均F1分数，显著优于未使用蒸馏的基线模型（0.9537 ± 0.0351），具有统计显著性。学生模型仅保留4.94M参数和19.01Mb大小，参数量减少5.7倍和17.5倍，实现37.72 CPU FPS。

Conclusion: HumP-KD框架能显著提升火灾分类模型的效率，同时保持高准确性和鲁棒性，适合实时部署在资源受限硬件上。

Abstract: Real-time fire classification systems require models that are simultaneously accurate, computationally efficient, and deployable on resource-constrained hardware. This work proposes \textbf{HumP-KD}, a Hybrid Uncertainty-aware Multi-stage Progressive Knowledge Distillation framework for efficient fire classification. Two datasets, FlameVision and Dataset-II, containing 8,600 and 31,309 images, are used. Various CNN and transformer baselines are applied under standard preprocessing, online augmentation, Gaussian noise and motion blur robustness conditions. The proposed HumP-KD model distills knowledge from two frozen heterogeneous transformer teachers, Swin-Tiny and ViT-Base, along with their Meta-MLP ensemble, into a lightweight MobileViT-S student via three tightly integrated components. Hierarchical Progressive Knowledge Distillation employs a Hierarchical Feature Builder. It generates a fused spatial attention mask to guide distillation toward discriminative regions selectively. Multi-Stage Knowledge Distillation progressively activates three distillation stages across training. On Dataset-II, HumP-KD achieves a mean F1 score of $0.9876 \pm 0.0063$ across 10 independent trials, significantly outperforming the MobileViT-S baseline trained without distillation ($0.9537 \pm 0.0351$), with statistical significance confirmed by both independent t-test ($p = 0.0195$) and Wilcoxon signed-rank test ($W = 1$, $p = 0.0039$). The proposed method also demonstrates strong generalization across datasets and robustness under degraded visual conditions. The student model retains only 4.94M parameters and 19.01Mb model size, representing a $5.7\times$ parameter reduction over Swin-Tiny and a $17.5\times$ reduction over ViT-Base, while achieving 37.72 CPU FPS, making it suitable for real-time deployment.

</details>


### [65] [CottonLeafVision: An Explainable and Robust Deep Learning Framework for Cotton Leaf Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14686)
*Rafi Ahamed,Md. Abir Rahman,Tasnia Tarannum Roza,Munaia Jannat Easha,Md. Asif Khan,Sudeepta Mandal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出“CottonLeafVision”系统，利用预训练深度学习模型准确识别棉花叶片病害，在公开数据集上达到98%的分类准确率，并开发了实际应用原型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 棉花是全球经济价值极高的作物，纺织业对其高度依赖。因此，准确识别和检测棉花叶片病害对于维持经济稳定至关重要。

Method: 研究评估了DenseNet201、InceptionV3、VGG19等多种预训练深度卷积神经网络模型，使用了一个公开的棉花叶片病害图像数据集（包含6种病害和1个健康类别，共7类，数据采集于真实田间环境）。为增强模型的可靠性和可解释性，采用了梯度加权类激活映射（Grad-CAM）、遮挡敏感性分析以及对抗训练等方法来提高模型的抗噪声能力。

Result: 在评估的预训练模型中，DenseNet201表现最优，实现了98%的最高分类准确率。同时，所采用的可解释性技术（如Grad-CAM）有效提升了模型的可理解性，对抗训练增强了模型的鲁棒性。最终，研究开发了一个原型系统，以将模型的能力应用于实际农业生产中。

Conclusion: 本研究展示了深度学习模型在真实场景下对棉花病害进行分类的能力，为实际农业病害管理提供了有效的技术工具和解决方案。

Abstract: Globally, cotton is a highly economically beneficial crop, as the textile industry heavily depends on it. So, the precise identification and detection of cotton leaf disease is crucial for economic stability. The development goal of "CottonLeafVision" is to accurately classify and detect cotton leaf disease. With this goal, we have evaluated multiple pretrained Deep Convolutional Neural Networks, including DenseNet201, InceptionV3, and VGG19 on a publicly available cotton leaf disease image dataset. This image dataset includes seven classes, six disease classes, and one healthy class, collected under various field conditions reflecting real-world challenges. Among these pretrained models, with DenseNet201, we have achieved the highest classification accuracy of 98%. To enhance the model reliability and interpretability, we have implemented different techniques and methods such as Gradient-weighted Class Activation Mapping (Grad-CAM), occlusion sensitivity analysis and adversarial training to increase the noise resistance of the model. Finally, we have developed a prototype in order to utilize the model's capabilities on real life agriculture. This paper shows the deep learning model's capabilities to classify the disease in real-life cotton disease management situations.

</details>


### [66] [ClinHallu: A Benchmark for Diagnosing Stage-Wise Hallucinations in Medical MLLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14697)
*Sicheng Yang,Hangjie Yuan,Wenjun Zhang,Jinwang Wang,Yichen Qian,Weihua Chen,Fan Wang,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了ClinHallu基准，用于诊断医疗多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）推理过程中的分阶段幻觉。该基准通过结构化推理轨迹和阶段替换干预，能够细粒度地定位幻觉来源，并展示了通过轨迹监督微调可以有效减少幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建可信的医疗多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）是实现可靠临床决策支持的关键。现有的医疗幻觉基准主要关注数据收集，但往往忽略了幻觉在模型推理过程中的具体起源。研究发现，幻觉来源因样本而异，可能源于视觉信息误识别、医学知识回忆错误，或是推理整合阶段的缺陷。因此，需要一种能够诊断推理过程中具体阶段幻觉的评估方法。

Method: 1. 提出并构建了ClinHallu基准，包含7,031个经过验证的实例。
2. 为每个实例标注了结构化的推理轨迹，并将其分解为三个关键阶段：视觉识别、知识回忆和推理整合。
3. 设计了阶段替换干预实验，通过修正特定阶段的输出，来量化该阶段错误对最终答案准确性的具体影响。
4. 在评估之外，探索了利用带有阶段标注的推理轨迹进行监督微调，以减少模型在各阶段的幻觉。

Result: 1. 阶段替换干预实验表明，模型在视觉识别、知识回忆和推理整合各阶段都可能引入错误，且不同样本的主导错误源不同。
2. 实验结果证明，使用结构化的推理轨迹进行监督微调，能够有效降低模型在各个推理阶段产生幻觉的频率，从而提升答案的可靠性。
3. ClinHallu作为一个细粒度的测试基准，能够精准诊断医疗MLLMs的推理失败环节。

Conclusion: ClinHallu为医疗多模态大语言模型的幻觉问题提供了一个细粒度、阶段化的诊断与评估基准。它不仅揭示了幻觉在推理链中的具体来源，还通过轨迹监督微调展示了缓解幻觉的有效途径。该基准已公开发布，为后续研究提供了重要工具，有助于推动更可靠、更可信的医疗AI系统的发展。

Abstract: Building trustworthy medical multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is critical for reliable clinical decision support. Existing medical hallucination benchmarks mainly focus on data collection, but often ignore where hallucinations originate within the reasoning process. We find that hallucination sources vary across samples: errors may arise from visual misrecognition, incorrect medical knowledge recall, or flawed reasoning integration. To enable source-level hallucination diagnosis, we introduce ClinHallu, a benchmark for stage-wise hallucination diagnosis in medical MLLM reasoning. ClinHallu contains 7,031 validated instances, where each instance is augmented with a structured reasoning trace decomposed into Visual Recognition, Knowledge Recall, and Reasoning Integration. We also use stage-replacement interventions to measure how correcting specific stages affects the final answer. Beyond evaluation, we show that trace-supervised fine-tuning reduces stage-wise hallucinations. ClinHallu provides a fine-grained hallucination testbed for diagnosing and mitigating reasoning failures in medical MLLMs. The benchmark is publicly available at https://github.com/alibaba-damo-academy/ClinHallu.

</details>


### [67] [RepFusion: Leveraging Multimodal Priors for Denoising in Representation Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14700)
*Xichen Pan,Aashu Singh,Satya Narayan Shukla,Xiangjun Fan,Shlok Kumar Mishra,Saining Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RepFusion方法，将多模态大型语言模型（MLLM）重新用作带噪声的视觉表示编码器，并用其输出作为扩散变换器的条件信号，从而在文生图任务中有效利用MLLM的先验知识进行去噪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图系统通常将LLM限制为文本编码器，而去噪由新训练的生成骨干负责。表示自编码器的出现使生成目标转向语义结构化的视觉表示，创造了与预训练LLM先验更兼容的潜在空间。受MLLM启发，其中仅用MLP投影器即可将干净视觉表示与预训练LLM对齐，但现有方法未充分利用MLLM处理带噪声输入的能力。因此，研究旨在探索如何将MLLM的先验知识扩展到带噪声的视觉表示去噪任务中。

Method: 研究提出RepFusion，将MLLM本身重新用作带噪声的表示编码器。具体方法是：将MLLM的机制从处理干净输入扩展到处理带噪声输入，使其输出成为带噪声视觉表示的语义条件信号；然后，将这些MLLM输出作为条件信号输入扩散变换器（Diffusion Transformer）进行图像生成。

Result: 在相似推理预算下的控制对比实验中，RepFusion优于那些将相当容量分配给新初始化去噪器的基线方法。这表明MLLM为去噪视觉表示提供了强大的先验知识。

Conclusion: 研究表明，MLLM蕴含强大的视觉表示去噪先验；通过基于演化中带噪声表示进行条件生成，测试时的计算资源可以有效地用于现代文生图系统中重复的MLLM条件生成过程，从而提升生成质量和效率。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used in text-to-image (T2I) systems, but they are typically limited to text encoding, while denoising is handled by newly trained generative backbones. The emergence of representation autoencoders (RAEs) shifts the generation target toward semantically structured visual representations, creating a latent space that is more compatible with pretrained LLM priors. Inspired by multimodal LLMs (MLLMs), where an MLP projector is sufficient to align clean visual representations with a pretrained LLM, we repurpose the MLLM itself as a noisy representation encoder, extending this mechanism from clean to noisy inputs. We present RepFusion, which uses the resulting MLLM outputs as the conditioning signal for a diffusion transformer. In controlled comparisons at similar inference budgets, RepFusion outperforms baselines that devote comparable capacity to newly initialized denoisers. These results demonstrate that MLLMs provide strong priors for denoising visual representations and that, by conditioning on evolving noisy representations, test-time compute can be productively spent on repeated MLLM conditioning in modern T2I systems.

</details>


### [68] [RATS! Patches Talk Through Registers: Emergent Parts in Register Attention Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14701)
*Timing Yang,Predrag Neskovic,Jansen Seheult,Wenchao Han,Anand Bhattad,Alan Yuille,Feng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RATS（Register Attention Transformers）模型，通过将分类标记分解为多个可学习的寄存器令牌，并利用压缩-通信-广播三步注意力机制，使模型在自监督学习中能够自发发现视觉数据中的组合结构（如物体部件），无需额外标注，且在多个分割任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉系统能够识别物体的结构化部件（如鸟的头、翅膀、爪子），但现有的自监督视觉模型通常仅学习整体表示，缺乏对部件级别组合结构的发现能力。因此，本研究旨在设计一种架构，使模型能像人类一样自主学习物体的可重用部件结构，从而提升视觉表示的结构化和可解释性。

Method: RATS将标准的分类标记分解为N个可学习的寄存器令牌，通过一个三步注意力机制（压缩、通信、广播）路由图像补丁信息。具体来说，寄存器被分配到不同的注意力头，彼此独立，从而每个寄存器专注于学习特定的原型语义区域，无需辅助损失或部件标注。

Result: 在五个分割基准测试中，RATS平均超过基线+12 mIoU；在ADE20K数据集上提升+1.11 mIoU，在COCO数据集上提升+0.2 AP^m。此外，学习到的寄存器词典在相关类别中表现出部件级一致性和语义接近性，验证了其自动发现物体部件的能力。

Conclusion: RATS通过引入寄存器令牌和特定注意力机制，提供了一种结构化且可解释的视觉表示学习的架构先验。该模型能够在自监督设置下自发发现物体的组合结构（部件），无需任何部件标注，并在下游任务中取得显著性能提升，表明这种架构设计有助于学习更结构化和可解释的视觉表示。

Abstract: When humans see a bird, they recognize far more than just "bird" -- they see a head, wings, and talons, a structured assembly of reusable parts that can be identified across every bird they have ever seen. We ask whether a self-supervised visual model can discover the same compositional structure on its own. To this end, we propose RATS (Register Attention Transformers), which decomposes the classification token into N learnable register tokens that route patch information through an L->N->N->L bottleneck via a three-step compress-communicate-broadcast attention. The N registers are partitioned across the H attention heads, so that registers assigned to different heads do not interact with each other. Without auxiliary losses or part annotations, each register spontaneously specializes into a proto-semantic region whose emerging structure resembles object parts. RATS surpasses all baselines by +12 mIoU on average across five segmentation benchmarks, with consistent gains on ADE20K (+1.11 mIoU) and COCO (+0.2 AP^m). Its register dictionary further exhibits part-level consistency and semantic proximity across related categories. Our results suggest that RATS may provide a useful architectural prior for structured and interpretable visual representation learning.

</details>


### [69] [OmniVideo-100K: A Dataset for Audio-Visual Reasoning through Structured Scripts and Evidence Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14702)
*Xinyue Cai,Chaoyou Fu,Yi-Fan Zhang,Ran He,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对现有视听问答自动化流水线在音视频关联、跨片段一致性和深层推理方面的不足，本文提出一个包含实体锚定脚本化和线索引导生成的新数据引擎，并构建了OmniVideo-100K数据集，显著提升了模型的性能和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有“视频-描述-问答”范式存在三大缺陷：1）音频与视觉模态独立处理，切断了其固有联系；2）视频片段独立处理导致对同一实体的描述不一致；3）将长文本理解与问答生成耦合到一步，限制了模型进行长期时间连接和深度跨模态推理的能力。

Method: 提出一个自动化数据引擎，包含两个核心机制：1）实体锚定视频脚本化：将视频转化为结构化脚本（含摘要、主实体列表和分段音视频描述），利用实体列表作为全局先验确保跨片段指代一致性和重建音视频关联；2）线索引导问答生成：先提示模型从脚本中挖掘跨片段、多模态线索，再基于这些高价值线索生成问答对。

Result: 基于该流水线构建了指令微调数据集OmniVideo-100K和人工验证测试集OmniVideo-Test。在OmniVideo-100K上对VITA-1.5、Qwen2.5-Omni-7B和Qwen3-Omni-30B进行微调，在OmniVideo-Test上取得了高达20.59%的性能提升，并在Daily-Omni和JointAVBench等基准测试中展现了强大的泛化能力（最高提升12.64%）。

Conclusion: 所提出的自动化数据引擎有效解决了现有视听问答数据生成流水线的关键缺陷，通过构建高质量的OmniVideo-100K数据集，能够显著提升多模态模型在复杂视听问答任务上的性能与跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Current automated pipelines for audio-visual Question Answering (QA) generally adopt a ``video-caption-QA'' paradigm. However, these methods typically segment videos into short clips and generate separate descriptions for audio and visual modalities. This decoupled processing severs inherent associations between sounds and their visual sources, while independent clip processing often causes inconsistent descriptions of the same entity across segments. Furthermore, coupling long-text comprehension and QA synthesis into a single step often restricts models to localized events, yielding questions lacking long-term temporal connections and deep cross-modal reasoning. To address these issues, we propose an automated data engine featuring two mechanisms: (1) \textbf{Entity-Anchored Video Scripting} transforms videos into structured scripts, comprising summaries, main entity lists, and segment-wise audio-visual descriptions. The entity list serves as a global prior to ensure cross-segment referential consistency and reconstruct audio-visual associations. (2) \textbf{Clue-Guided QA Generation} prompts models to first mine cross-segment, multimodal clues from the script, and subsequently generate QA pairs based on these high-value clues. Leveraging this pipeline, we construct the instruction-tuning dataset \textbf{OmniVideo-100K} and a human-verified test set, \textbf{OmniVideo-Test}. Fine-tuning VITA-1.5, Qwen2.5-Omni-7B and Qwen3-Omni-30B on OmniVideo-100K yields performance gains of up to 20.59% on OmniVideo-Test, demonstrating strong generalization (up to 12.64% improvements) across established benchmarks like Daily-Omni and JointAVBench.

</details>


### [70] [Gaze Heads: How VLMs Look at What They Describe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14703)
*Rohit Gandikota,David Bau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究发现视觉语言模型中存在特定的“凝视注意力头”机制，能够跟踪图像描述区域，通过定向干预这些注意力头可以在推理时引导模型行为，无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究视觉语言模型如何在内部解决图像描述任务，理解其内部机制并寻找可操控的推理时引导方法。

Method: 使用漫画条作为受控测试平台，通过相关性评分识别模型中的“凝视注意力头”；通过注意力掩膜干预实验验证这些头的功能；在不同模型规模和架构上测试机制的普遍性。

Result: 成功识别出负责跟踪描述区域的凝视注意力头；对前100个此类头进行干预能以83.1%准确率重定向模型回答到指定区域；该机制在2B-32B参数模型和多种VLM架构中普遍存在，但在某些冻结编码器家族中缺失。

Conclusion: 通过机制分析识别的注意力头定向干预可作为实用推理时杠杆，无需重新训练即可引导多模态模型行为，为模型行为控制提供了新途径。

Abstract: How a vision-language model internally solves the task of describing an image is far from obvious. We find that the model develops a specific mechanism for this: a small set of attention heads in its language-model backbone, which we call gaze heads, whose attention tracks the image region the model is currently describing. We find them with a simple correlation score from a few forward passes, using comic strips as a controlled testbed where narrative order is laid out spatially. These gaze heads do not just track the image tokens being described: redirecting their attention to a chosen region forces the VLM to describe that region instead. A single attention-mask intervention on the top-100 gaze heads, fewer than 9% of all heads, steers the model's answer to any chosen comic panel at 83.1% accuracy, while the same intervention on random heads fails to redirect the answer, and intervening on all heads destroys generation. The same lever also extends to continuous control: switching the gaze target mid-generation makes the model wrap up its current panel description and move to the new one within a few tokens. Beyond comics, the same intervention redirects answers to chosen regions in natural COCO images. The mechanism further recurs across model sizes from 2B to 32B parameters and across other VLM architectures, although some frozen-encoder families show no comparable head set. More broadly, this shows that targeted edits identified through mechanistic analysis can serve as practical inference-time levers for steering multimodal model behavior, without any retraining. Our code, interactive demo, and datasets are available at https://gaze.baulab.info/

</details>

### [71] [Occupancy-Grounded Room Segmentation for Hierarchical 3D Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13727)
*Carlos Cueto Zumaya,Iacopo Catalano,Jorge Peña-Queralta,Wallace Moreira Bessa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于占用空间的室内3D场景图构建方法，为房间节点提供明确的多边形足迹。相比基线方法，能恢复更多房间实例但精度较低，准确的房间边界仍是开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内机器人3D场景图系统在不同空间基底（如地点聚类、墙面平面或分割结果）上构建房间层，导致房间节点缺乏统一的几何评估标准。

Method: 采用占用空间分解技术，将房间节点锚定到从占用空间派生并跟踪的自由空间区域，为每个房间生成显式的多边形足迹。

Result: 在12个Matterport3D场景上的评估表明，与基于地点连接性的基线Hydra相比，占用空间锚定方法能恢复出更多的房间实例，但精度较低；准确的房间边界构建对两种方法而言均仍是一个开放问题。

Conclusion: 基于占用空间的锚定方法能为3D场景图中的房间节点提供更一致、更几何显式的表示，相比现有基线能发现更多房间实例。然而，提高房间边界的精度是未来需要解决的关键挑战。

Abstract: Hierarchical 3D scene graphs (3DSGs) for indoor robots organize geometric and semantic information across spatial scales, with a room layer that connects object-level perception to room-scale reasoning. Existing systems construct this layer from different spatial substrates (\eg{} place clusters, wall planes, or segmentation outputs), and as a result, room nodes are not evaluated on a common geometric criterion. We present an occupancy-grounded 3DSG pipeline in which room nodes are anchored to tracked free-space regions derived from occupancy decomposition, giving each room an explicit polygonal footprint. We evaluate the pipeline on 12 Matterport3D scenes by matching predicted room polygons to annotated room instances and compare against Hydra, a representative state-of-the-art place-connectivity baseline. The results show that occupancy-grounded anchoring recovers substantially more room instances than place-connectivity construction, at the cost of lower precision, and that wall-accurate room boundaries remain an open problem for both methods. Code is available at https://github.com/crcz25/OccuSG.

</details>


### [72] [Scalable Dynamic Tactile Sensing Enabled by Passive and Flexible Acoustic Waveguides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13746)
*Guimin Long,Changhong Linghu,Chuanping Liu,Ke Xu,Xingjian Jing*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于深亚波长声波导的被动分布式触觉传感范式，通过亥姆霍兹谐振器网络和稀疏麦克风嵌入，解决了大规模柔性阵列中灵敏度、鲁棒性与顺应性之间的性能权衡问题，实现了快速、精确的触觉信号定位与重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人工动态触觉传感技术难以同时满足灵敏度、鲁棒性和顺应性要求，尤其在扩展到大面积柔性阵列时，面临性能下降、布线复杂、成本高昂等挑战。本研究旨在开发一种能将性能与结构灵活性解耦的新型传感架构。

Method: 1. 设计被动分布式声波导网络：由弹性膜封顶的亥姆霍兹谐振器和弹簧增强微管构成封闭网络，在宏观弯曲下保持声学传输特性不变。
2. 采用稀疏嵌入式麦克风阵列进行信号采集。
3. 信号处理与推理算法：结合快速连续小波变换（Fast CWT）与轻量级神经网络，实现实时信号处理和模式识别。

Result: 1. 空间定位精度：最高达4毫米。
2. 定位准确率：在4个麦克风、64个节点的传感阵列中超过99%。
3. 信号重建：能重建低于100 Hz的低频波形。
4. 推理速度：在5.5毫秒内完成信号推理。
5. 原型验证：成功开发了指尖阵列、触觉手套和大面积皮肤贴片等可贴合原型，能检测从单根毛发接触到5毫克微粒冲击、动脉搏动、羽毛触碰和手指接触等多种微小刺激。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了一种可扩展、灵活且低成本的被动分布式触觉传感新范式，成功克服了现有技术的大规模扩展瓶颈。该技术为下一代高分辨率、可贴合的人机交互界面提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Artificial dynamic tactile sensing requires sensitivity, robustness, and compliance, yet existing technologies face trade-offs when scaling to large-area arrays, compounded by wiring complexity and cost. Here, we report a passive distributed paradigm using deep sub-wavelength acoustic waveguides that decouples performance from structural flexibility. Elastic-membrane-capped Helmholtz resonators interconnected by spring-reinforced microtubes form an enclosed network with invariant acoustic transmission under macroscopic bending. By sparsely embedding microphones, the system achieves real-time localization (4 mm highest spatial resolution; >99% accuracy in a 4 microphones 64-node sensing array) and waveform reconstruction of low-frequency signals (<100 Hz). Fast Continuous Wavelet Transform and a lightweight neural network enable inference within 5.5 ms. We demonstrate conformable prototypes-fingertip arrays, a tactile glove, and large-area skins-detecting stimuli from single-hair contact to 5-mg particle impacts, arterial pulse waves, feather touches, and finger contact. This establishes a scalable, flexible, low-cost paradigm for next-generation human-machine interfaces.

</details>


### [73] [$μ_0$: A Scalable 3D Interaction-Trace World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13769)
*Seungjae Lee,Yoonkyo Jung,Jusuk Lee,Jonghun Shin,Amir Hossein Shahidzadeh,Yao-Chih Lee,H. Jin Kim,Jia-Bin Huang,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于3D轨迹（traces）的可扩展世界模型μ₀，通过预测关键交互点的平滑3D轨迹（而非密集像素或具身特定动作），实现跨具身、可迁移的机器人操控学习。其配套的自动3D监督提取系统（TraceExtract）使模型能从多样视频中无监督预训练，实验表明其性能优于基线模型，并可与动作专家结合，在无动作监督下达到与有监督模型（如π₀）竞争的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人世界模型面临关键权衡：像素级视频模型虽具广泛视觉先验，但过度消耗模型容量于外观重建；直接动作模型则依赖具身特定标签，限制了可扩展性和跨具身迁移。需要一种既能抽象表示物理变化、又无需具身特定动作监督的可扩展表示方法。

Method: 提出μ₀模型：1) 表示层面：仅预测物体、工具、手、接触区域等关键交互点的未来平滑3D轨迹（traces），形成紧凑的、与具身无关的运动接口。2) 监督提取：开发TraceExtract系统自动从多样视频中提取3D监督，包括关键点选择、全局对齐轨迹构建、以及将运动片段与层次化语言描述关联。3) 模型架构：结合预训练视觉-语言骨干网络与模块化“轨迹专家”（trace expert），后者将每个查询表示为B样条控制点并预测未来轨迹，通过TraceExtract提供的监督进行预训练。

Result: 1) 预测性能：在2D和3D轨迹预测任务上，μ₀均超越现有基线（包括专用轨迹预测模型和分词视觉语言模型）。2) 跨具身应用：μ₀预训练后保持冻结并可重用，通过与下游具身的动作专家结合，形成的轨迹条件策略在性能上可与经过动作监督预训练的视觉-语言-动作模型（如π₀）相竞争。3) 验证了3D轨迹作为跨具身操控表示的可行性。

Conclusion: 3D轨迹（traces）是一种可扩展、可迁移的物理交互表示，能够在无需具身特定动作标签的情况下实现有效的机器人操控学习。μ₀模型通过预测关键点的轨迹，成功构建了一个与具身无关的世界模型，为跨具身机器人学习和迁移提供了新的范式。

Abstract: World models that capture how actions induce physical change enable scalable robot learning without reliance on embodiment-specific action labels. Pixel-space video models provide broad visual priors but expend model capacity on dense appearance reconstruction, while direct action models require embodiment-specific labels that hinder scalability. We present $μ_0$, a scalable world model based on 3D traces. Rather than predicting dense pixels or directly modeling actions, $μ_0$ forecasts smooth 3D trajectories for salient interaction points such as objects, tools, hands, and contact regions, yielding a compact, embodiment-agnostic motion interface. To enable training from diverse video sources, our TraceExtract system automatically extracts 3D supervision by selecting keypoints, constructing globally aligned traces, and associating motion segments with hierarchical language captions. This TraceExtract supervision pretrains $μ_0$ by combining a pretrained vision-language backbone with a modular trace expert, which represents each query via B-spline control points and predicts future traces. Experiments show that $μ_0$ outperforms baselines in both 2D and 3D trace prediction, including trace prediction models and tokenized VLM methods. Because $μ_0$ is frozen and reusable, it can be paired with action experts for downstream robot embodiments. Despite action-free pretraining, the resulting trace-conditioned policies achieve performance competitive with VLA models pretrained with action supervision, such as $π_0$. These results establish 3D traces as a scalable and transferable representation for cross-embodiment manipulation.

</details>


### [74] [FlowMo-WM: A World Model with Object Momentum and Hidden Ambient Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13817)
*Yitao Jiang,Luyang Zhao,Muhao Chen,Devin Balkcom*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FlowMo-WM，一种端到端可训练的视觉世界模型，能够从图像-动作历史中推断物体运动状态和预测隐藏漂移，无需直接监督流场，在模拟水表面车辆环境中提升了长时域滚动预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习中的许多动作条件世界模型在运动主要受即时控制主导的设置中进行评估，但在水表面车辆等真实世界物体中，运动受惯性影响，并受隐藏环境漂移（如水流、风）的影响。这使得现有模型在预测此类物体运动时面临挑战。

Method: FlowMo-WM将图像-动作历史分解为：1）短期潜在状态，用于总结物体运动；2）更长期的上下文，用于总结缓慢变化的外部影响。在潜在滚动过程中，采用零上下文残差转换，将动作条件基础动力学与上下文相关的漂移效应分离。模型为端到端可训练。

Result: 在具有多样化隐藏流、扰动和随机车辆动力学的模拟水表面车辆环境中，FlowMo-WM相较于代表性动作条件潜在世界模型，提高了长时域滚动预测的准确性。通过预测时上下文消融实验表明，环境上下文对于在隐藏漂移下的稳定预测很重要。冻结的线性探针表征了学习到的因素中编码的信息。

Conclusion: FlowMo-WM通过有效分离物体运动和外部漂移效应，并利用历史上下文信息，解决了传统世界模型在具有惯性和隐藏环境影响场景中的局限性，提升了预测准确性。该模型无需直接监督流场，能够推断相关状态，为在复杂真实环境中的机器人控制与规划提供了更可靠的预测工具。

Abstract: World models in robot learning predict future states from visual observations and actions, enabling agents to reason about the consequences of their controls. However, many action-conditioned models are evaluated in settings where motion is dominated by immediate control, whereas aquatic surface vehicles and other real-world objects continue moving under inertia and are displaced by hidden ambient drift, such as water currents or wind. We propose FlowMo-WM, an end-to-end trainable visual world model that infers object-centric motion state and a predictive long-history context associated with hidden drift from image-action histories without direct supervision of flow fields. FlowMo-WM factorizes image-action history into a short-history latent state, trained to summarize object-centric motion, and a longer-history context, trained to summarize slowly varying exogenous influences. A zero-context residual transition separates action-conditioned base dynamics from context-dependent drift effects during latent rollout. In simulated aquatic surface-vehicle environments with diverse hidden flows, disturbances, and randomized vehicle dynamics, FlowMo-WM improves long-horizon rollout accuracy over representative action-conditioned latent world models. Prediction-time context ablations, in which the inferred context is zeroed or shuffled during rollout, show that the ambient context is important for stable prediction under hidden drift, while frozen linear probes characterize information encoded in the learned factors.

</details>


### [75] [Multi-Agent Embodied Autonomous Driving: From V2X Information Exchange to Shared World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13840)
*Senkang Hu,Zhengru Fang,Yihang Tao,Zihan Fang,Sam Tak Wu Kwong,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 这篇综述探讨了自动驾驶如何通过共享世界模型从单车智能转向多智能体协作系统，系统总结了380多篇文献的研究现状，涵盖了V2X通信、协作感知、协同规划等多个领域，并指出了当前评估方法和实时安全保障方面的研究缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶正从孤立的车辆智能向多智能体系统演变，这需要跨车辆、基础设施和其他交通参与者的共享预测性表征。然而，当前研究在如何将交换的观察转化为对齐的状态、意图感知交互和协调的下游行动方面存在不足，特别是在可验证的共享状态维护、鲁棒的意图对齐和安全协调行动方面缺乏深入探索。

Method: 通过系统综述380多篇文献，涵盖车对万物通信、协作感知、智能体间认知、协同规划、端到端协同驾驶以及用于闭环验证的仿真和数据引擎等多个研究方向，采用文献分析法对现有研究进行系统梳理和分类总结。

Result: 研究发现当前评估主要集中在仿真环境、定制基准测试和离线协议上，缺乏真实场景下的验证。基于基础模型的协调在开放交通环境中缺乏经过验证的实时安全保障，这表明现有技术在实际部署方面仍面临重大挑战。

Conclusion: 这些研究缺口指明了多智能体自动驾驶系统（MAEAD）的关键研究方向：需要发展可验证的共享状态维护机制、建立鲁棒的意图和计划对齐方法，以及实现通信、延迟和部署约束下的安全协调行动。未来的研究应重点关注这些方面的突破，以推动多智能体自动驾驶系统的实际应用。

Abstract: Autonomous driving is shifting from isolated vehicle intelligence toward multi-agent embodied systems that share perception, infer intent, and coordinate action under uncertainty. This survey examines this transition through the lens of Shared World Models (SWMs): predictive cross-agent representations maintained across vehicles, infrastructure, and other traffic participants. We review more than 380 publications spanning vehicle-to-everything (V2X) communication, collaborative perception, inter-agent cognition, cooperative planning, end-to-end cooperative driving, and simulation and data engines for closed-loop validation. The organizing question is how exchanged observations become aligned state, intent-aware interaction, and coordinated downstream action. Across the surveyed literature, evaluation remains concentrated in simulation, curated benchmarks, and offline protocols. Foundation-model-based coordination also lacks verified real-time safety guarantees in open traffic. These gaps motivate key research priorities for multi-agent embodied autonomous driving (MAEAD): verifiable shared-state maintenance, robust intent and plan alignment, and safe coordinated action under communication, latency, and deployment constraints.

</details>


### [76] [Efficient Domain-Adaptive Policy Learning via Kernel Representation with Application to Quadrotor Control under Non-Stationary Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13842)
*Hongyu Zhou,Mingtian Tan,Vasileios Tzoumas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于核表示的高效域自适应策略学习算法，利用随机傅里叶特征（RFF）近似核函数来建模非平稳环境扰动。该方法在离线训练中随机采样核参数以生成多样化的扰动模型，并通过可微仿真优化策略；在线部署时，通过实时最小二乘估计更新核系数和带宽，实现快速自适应。实验在四旋翼轨迹跟踪任务中验证了其高效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实环境中部署机器人策略时，需要处理如风、载荷变化等非平稳扰动。现有方法往往难以在表达复杂“仿真-现实”差距和保持在线适应计算效率之间取得平衡。本文旨在开发一种既能灵活建模复杂扰动，又能支持快速在线适应的策略学习方法。

Method: 算法核心是使用随机傅里叶特征（RFF）对核函数进行可微近似。1）离线阶段：在仿真中，随机采样核系数和带宽参数，生成大量多样化的扰动场景；利用可微仿真和解析梯度优化控制策略。2）在线阶段：部署时，通过在线最小二乘估计实时更新核系数和带宽，以适应非平稳环境。

Result: 该方法在RTX 4090 GPU上训练时间仅需50秒，计算效率高。在四旋翼（Crazyflie）的轨迹跟踪任务中，通过高保真仿真和硬件实验验证：算法能有效应对复杂气动效应、风、地面效应及载荷变化等多种扰动，表现出良好的域自适应能力和实时性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于核表示与随机傅里叶特征的域自适应策略学习方法，成功平衡了模型的表达能力与计算效率。它能够快速训练并实时适应非平稳环境扰动，为机器人在复杂真实环境中的部署提供了一种高效、鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: We present an algorithm for efficient domain-adaptive policy learning via kernel representations. Learning domain-adaptive policies is challenging since it requires an environment representation that is both sufficiently expressive to model complex sim-to-real gaps during offline training, and computationally efficient enough to support rapid online adaptation during deployment. For instance, a quadrotor may encounter time-varying, non-stationary disturbances, such as sudden gusts of wind, payload shifts, or transitions between distinct flight regimes with and without ground effects. To address these challenges, we model unknown disturbances using a differentiable kernel approximation based on random Fourier features. During the offline training phase, we randomly sample kernel coefficients and bandwidth parameters to generate a rich diversity of disturbance profiles. We then optimize the control policy via differentiable simulation with analytical gradients, a process that takes only 50 seconds of training time on an RTX 4090 GPU. During hardware deployment, the policy adapts to non-stationary environments in real time by updating both the kernel coefficients and bandwidth through online least-squares estimation. We evaluate our method on quadrotor trajectory tracking tasks across high-fidelity numerical simulations and hardware experiments using Crazyflie, subjected to various disturbances, including complex aerodynamic effects, wind, ground effects, and payload fluctuations.

</details>


### [77] [Output-Level Regularization Eliminates the Seed Lottery in Single-GPU VLA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13856)
*Jeffrin Sam,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 微调视觉-语言-动作模型时，相同的训练代码和数据仅因随机种子不同，可能导致性能从91-94%大幅下降至65.2%，此现象称为“种子彩票”。根源在于动作预测器发生“输出崩溃”，产生几乎相同的输出。使用输出级正则化器（如VICReg、Dropout、降低学习率）可有效解决，而权重级方法无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单个GPU上微调视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）时，发现使用相同的代码、数据和架构，仅随机种子不同会导致性能出现巨大差异，且无法预测哪个种子会失败。现有的权重级正则化方法（如L2、EWC）对此问题无效，因此需要研究其根本原因并找到解决方案。

Method: 通过多次运行（最多13个随机种子）在3个LIBERO基准上的微调实验，观察性能差异。分析发现问题源于“输出崩溃”。比较了7种方法，包括输出级正则化器（VICReg、Dropout、降低学习率）和权重级方法（L2、EWC），并使用统计检验验证效果。

Result: 基线方法中，13次运行有1次发生灾难性失败（成功率65.2%）。输出级正则化器（VICReg、Dropout、降低学习率）在总共21次运行中均未出现灾难性失败，而权重级方法（L2、EWC）无法消除此问题。统计检验显示显著差异（F(12,11)=28.7, p<0.001）。

Conclusion: “种子彩票”问题的根源是动作预测器的“输出崩溃”，权重级正则化方法因无法检测输出空间的崩溃而失效。简单的输出级正则化器，如调整优化器配置中的学习率，即可有效消除此问题，提高微调过程的稳定性和可靠性。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a vision-language-action model (VLA-JEPA) on a single GPU should be simple: load a pretrained checkpoint, run training, deploy. There is a hidden danger. Run the same fine-tuning code thirteen times -- same data, same architecture, different random seed -- and twelve runs produce a robot succeeding 91--94% of the time, while one run silently degrades to 65.2%: a 29 pp gap with no error message, no warning, and no way to predict which seed will fail. We call this the seed lottery. We trace the cause to output collapse: the action predictor quietly learns to produce nearly identical outputs regardless of what the robot sees. Existing weight-level methods (L2, EWC) are structurally blind to this collapse -- they penalize weight changes, but collapse occurs in directions weights can move freely without affecting outputs, a gap we formalize via the Jacobian null-space. Across 7 methods x up to 13 seeds x 3 LIBERO benchmarks, three output-level regularizers -- VICReg (n=12 seeds), Dropout (n=4), and a halved learning rate (n=5) -- each eliminate every catastrophic seed (0/21 combined collapses vs. 1/13 Baseline; F(12,11)=28.7, p<0.001), while weight-level methods (L2, EWC) preserve the lottery. The simplest fix is changing one number in your optimizer config.

</details>


### [78] [ContactWorld: What Matters in Vision-Tactile World Models for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13877)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Pokuang Zhou,Kaidi Zhang,Adeesh Desai,Temitope Amosa,Davood Soleymanzadeh,Jiuzhou Lei,Minghui Zheng,Yu She*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了ContactWorld基准和系统实证研究，旨在探究在接触丰富机器人操作任务中，哪些视觉触觉世界模型的表示属性能支持稳定的长时域规划。研究发现，空间结构化且时间连续的表示（如点云）以及跨模态兼容的表示（如结合点云与触觉力场）能显著提升规划性能，且触觉传感在长时域任务中尤为关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的机器人操作需要世界模型来处理复杂的接触动力学和多模态感官观测。然而，尚不清楚哪些表示属性能够从根本上支持在接触丰富环境中进行稳定的长时域规划。

Method: 作者构建了一个名为ContactWorld的基准，包含12个接触丰富的操作任务（如插入、拆卸、拧紧和探索性交互）。在此基础上，进行了系统性的实验，对不同的视觉触觉世界模型表示（如腕部视图、前视图、点云观测）和模态组合（如结合触觉力场表示）进行了广泛评估和比较。

Result: 1. 空间结构化且时间连续的表示规划性能最佳。2. 点云观测将平均规划成功率从腕部视图的20.7%和前视图的22.0%提升至32.1%。3. 触觉传感的有效性关键取决于跨模态表示兼容性，而非单纯的模态扩展。结合点云与触觉力场表示（后者能保留更丰富的空间结构和交互动力学）将性能进一步提升至36.1%，在所有任务中取得最优。4. 触觉传感在长时域规划目标下变得愈发重要，因为随着时间推移，累积的预测误差和接触不确定性会不断增加。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了在面向接触丰富机器人操作的视觉触觉世界模型中，表示的结构、多模态兼容性以及长时域鲁棒性的重要性。这为设计能够支持复杂接触交互的稳定规划系统提供了关键指导。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires world models to reason over complex contact dynamics from multimodal sensory observations. However, it remains unclear which representation properties fundamentally support stable long-horizon planning in contact-rich settings. In this paper, we present ContactWorld, a benchmark and systematic empirical study of vision-tactile world models spanning 12 contact-rich manipulation tasks, including insertion, disassembly, screwing, and exploratory interaction. Across extensive experiments, we find that representations that are both spatially structured and temporally continuous consistently achieve the strongest planning performance. In particular, point-cloud observations improve average planning success rates from 20.7% with wrist-view observations and 22.0% with front-view observations to 32.1%. We further find that the effectiveness of tactile sensing depends critically on cross-modal representation compatibility rather than modality scaling alone. Combining point-cloud observations with tactile force-field representations, which preserve richer spatial structure and interaction dynamics, further improves performance to 36.1%, yielding the strongest overall planning performance across all evaluated tasks. Moreover, tactile sensing becomes increasingly important under long-horizon planning objectives, where compounding prediction errors and contact uncertainty accumulate over time. Together, these findings highlight the importance of representation structure, multimodal compatibility, and long-horizon robustness in vision-tactile world models for contact-rich robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [79] [AnyGoal: Vision-Language Guided Multi-Agent Exploration for Training-Free Lifelong Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13878)
*MoniJesu James,Marcelino Julio Fernando,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为AnyGoal的训练免费的多机器人导航架构，它以视觉语言模型（VLM）为核心，结合前沿探索和2D高斯贝叶斯价值图进行终身学习和任务协调，在分布外场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端导航策略在分布外场景中性能急剧下降；模块化流水线（如Modular GOAT）受限于封闭集物体检测的召回率；3D快照记忆系统（如3D-Mem）会产生密集、视图相关的表示，维护成本高。需要一种能处理分布外场景、召回率高且易于维护的导航架构。

Method: 1. 以视觉语言模型（VLM）为核心，用于评估目标相关性。2. 引入2D高斯贝叶斯价值图（BVM），维护每个像素关于目标相关性的后验概率（均值, 方差），通过VLM分数经深度锥掩模进行精度加权融合来更新，并在子任务间不重置，实现终身证据积累。3. 前沿探索通过VLM-as-judge softmax和BVM上的贝叶斯上置信界限项的凸组合进行排序。4. 采用具有空间分离惩罚和承诺滞后性的贪婪分配器，在无集中控制器的情况下将前沿分配给智能体。

Result: 在完整的GOAT-Bench验证集未见划分（360个剧集，2669个子任务）上，在严格的物理机制下（离散步长0.25米，无瞬移，42度水平视场角）：1. 双智能体系统达到52.4%的子任务成功率（Subtask SR）和12.7%的成功加权路径长度（SPL），达到当时最优水平，比Modular GOAT（24.9%）提高了27.5个百分点。2. 单智能体AnyGoal达到41.9%的子任务成功率，表明性能提升源于决策架构本身。3. 四方面的感知消融研究表明，开放词汇检测器将主要失败模式从探索转变为目标验证。

Conclusion: AnyGoal是一种无需训练的多机器人导航架构，通过将VLM置于决策核心并结合贝叶斯价值图进行终身学习和智能体协调，有效解决了现有方法在分布外场景下的性能瓶颈和召回率限制问题，显著提升了导航性能。其设计展示了决策架构本身（而非端到端训练）在复杂导航任务中的重要性。

Abstract: End-to-end navigation policies trained on large simulation corpora degrade sharply when transferred to out-of-distribution scenes, categories, or goal modalities. Modular pipelines such as Modular GOAT are bottlenecked by closed-set object detection recall, while 3D snapshot-memory systems (e.g. 3D-Mem) accumulate dense, view-dependent representations that are heavy to maintain. We present AnyGoal, a training-free multi-robot architecture that places a Vision-Language Model (VLM) at the core of frontier-based exploration and coordinates agents through a shared 2D Gaussian Bayesian Value Map (BVM). The BVM maintains a per-pixel (mu, sigma^2) posterior over goal relevance, updated via precision-weighted fusion of VLM scores through a depth-cone mask, and is never reset between subtasks, yielding lifelong evidence accumulation. Frontiers are ranked by a convex blend of a VLM-as-judge softmax and a Bayesian UCB term on the BVM. A greedy allocator with spatial-separation penalty and commitment hysteresis distributes frontiers across agents without a centralized controller. On the full GOAT-Bench val unseen split (360 episodes, 2,669 subtasks), our dual-agent system achieves 52.4% Subtask SR at 12.7% SPL--state of the art under the strict physical regime (discrete 0.25 m steps, no teleportation, 42 deg HFOV) and a +27.5 pp improvement over Modular GOAT (24.9%). Single-agent AnyGoal achieves 41.9% Subtask SR, showing gains arise from the decision architecture. A four-way perception ablation shows that open-vocabulary detectors shift the dominant failure mode from exploration to goal verification.

</details>


### [80] [Guided Diffusion with Distilled Vision-Language Reliability for Aerial Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13883)
*Ivan Valuev,Iana Zhura,Valerii Serpiva,Didar Seyidov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于3D无人机导航的可靠性感知扩散规划器，通过引入场景级可靠性热图来识别并规避感知不可靠的区域（如玻璃、镜子等），显著提升了导航安全性，同时利用蒸馏技术保持了实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的管道式无人机导航方法存在误差传播、延迟累积和环境适应性差的问题。现有的端到端生成模型虽然能直接映射原始观察到轨迹，但无法识别观察中的不可靠区域（如玻璃、镜子、过曝表面），容易将其作为有效信息用于规划，导致安全隐患。因此，需要一种能够感知并规避不可靠观察的导航方法。

Method: 提出了一种可靠性感知的扩散规划器。该规划器在生成轨迹时，不仅考虑原始观察，还结合了一个场景级可靠性热图，该热图由轻量级网络生成，该网络通过蒸馏视觉语言模型的开放词汇推理能力得到，用于标记感知不可信的区域。为了泛化到未见环境，设计了一个可微分的两阶段ESDF成本函数，将物理障碍物（来自深度信息）和虚拟障碍物（来自不可靠区域）同等对待，引导去噪过程。

Result: 在仿真和真实四旋翼飞行器上的实验表明，该规划器比最先进的扩散基线规划器更安全：障碍物违规率从40.3%显著降低至9.6%，穿越区域的平均可靠性从0.588提升至0.925。消融实验证实，可靠性项是关键组件，去除它会导致平均可靠性从0.898降至0.783。此外，蒸馏技术使得框架运行速度比使用完整视觉语言模型快2倍。

Conclusion: 本文提出的可靠性感知扩散规划器通过有效识别和规避感知不可靠区域，显著提升了无人机导航的安全性。引入的可靠性热图是关键创新，而蒸馏技术确保了方法的实时性。该方法在复杂环境（如包含玻璃、镜子）中展现出优越的性能，为安全可靠的自主导航提供了新思路。

Abstract: Autonomous UAV navigation is conventionally solved by pipelines that separate perception, mapping, and planning into distinct stages, which propagates errors, accumulates latency, and requires environment-specific retuning. End-to-end generative models remove these interfaces by mapping raw observations directly to trajectories, but inherit a subtle failure mode: trained on clean data, they cannot recognise when an observation is unreliable, and treat degraded regions such as glass, mirrors, and overexposed surfaces as valid evidence for planning. We present a reliability-aware diffusion planner for 3D UAV navigation. It conditions trajectory generation on the observation together with a scene-level reliability heatmap that marks where perception cannot be trusted, produced by a lightweight network that distils the open-vocabulary reasoning of a vision-language model within the real-time planning budget. To generalise to unseen environments without retraining, we steer the denoising process with a differentiable two-stage ESDF cost that treats physical obstacles from depth and virtual obstacles from highly unreliable regions on equal footing. In simulation and on a real quadrotor, our planner produces markedly safer trajectories than a state-of-the-art diffusion baseline, reducing the obstacle-violation rate from 40.3% to 9.6% and raising the mean reliability of traversed regions from 0.588 to 0.925. Ablating the reliability term alone drops mean reliability from 0.898 to 0.783, confirming it as the decisive component, while distillation runs the framework up to 2 times faster than the full vision-language model.

</details>


### [81] [PhysVLA: Towards Physically-Grounded VLA for Embodied Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13886)
*Namai Chandra,Shriram Damodaran,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysVLA是一个无需重训练即可应用于任何视觉-语言-动作模型的推理时框架，通过双层物理修正机制弥补了标准VLA模型在物理一致性方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作模型主要基于行为演示数据进行训练，未能显式地施加刚体动力学或接触约束等基本物理原理，导致存在关键的物理鸿沟。仅通过时间平滑来修正单步或分块VLA输出的做法，会以牺牲轨迹质量为代价来避免短期记忆无法解决的失败。

Method: PhysVLA框架在推理时拦截VLA模型预测的控制动作，并应用双层修正：（1）基于相位感知的有限状态机，用于结构化离散任务阶段（如接近、抓取、搬运和放置）；（2）选择性欧拉-拉格朗日门，仅在动力学预言机检测到运动学/动力学不一致时激活。

Result: 在LIBERO-Spatial基准上对OpenVLA等多个VLA模型进行评估，PhysVLA实现了高达17%的绝对成功率提升和高达19%的稳定性提升，且无任务回退。在所有四个模型骨干上，轨迹效率提升高达15%。在Robosuite Lift的跨模拟器测试中，轨迹平滑度鲁棒性提升高达10倍。在真实的Agilex Piper机械臂上验证，无需重训练即可实现高达50%的成功率提升。

Conclusion: 物理感知能力可以作为一个可组合的、与骨干模型无关的推理时模块，无缝集成到现有的VLA模型中，显著提升机器人操作任务的性能、稳定性和轨迹质量，并能有效迁移到真实物理硬件。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models excel at mapping visual inputs and natural language instructions directly to robotic control policies. However, because they are trained primarily to fit behavioural demonstration data, they do not explicitly enforce fundamental physical principles such as rigid-body dynamics or contact constraints. This exposes a critical physics gap: standard temporal smoothing applied on top of single-step or chunked VLAs trades trajectory quality for added failures that short-term memory cannot resolve. To bridge this gap, we introduce PhysVLA (Physics-VLA), a plug-and-play, inference-time framework designed to wrap any frozen VLA backbone without retraining, fine-tuning, or weight access, with less than 1 ms of overhead per control step. PhysVLA intercepts the predicted control action, captures only the simulator or system state, and applies a dual-layered correction: (i) a phase-aware finite-state machine that structures discrete task segments (approach, grasp, transport, and place), and (ii) a selective Euler-Lagrange gate that activates only when a dynamics oracle detects kinodynamic inconsistency. Evaluated across OpenVLA, OpenVLA-OFT, Force-VLA, and Generalist-VLA on LIBERO-Spatial with a 7-DoF Franka Panda, the framework delivers absolute success rate increases of up to 17% and stability increases of up to 19% with no per-task regressions, improves trajectory efficiency by up to 15% across all four backbones, and shows up to a 10x improvement in trajectory jerk robustness on a Robosuite Lift cross-simulator sweep. We further validate the framework on a real Agilex Piper arm with a pick-and-place task, confirming that PhysVLA transfers to physical hardware without retraining, with success-rate improvements of up to 50%, establishing physical awareness as a composable, backbone-agnostic runtime module.

</details>


### [82] [Learning Dynamic Swing-Up of an Inverted Pendulum using Remote Magnetic Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13915)
*Viacheslav Sydora,Jasan Zughaibi,Denis von Arx,Quentin Boehler,Michael Muehlebach*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次使用临床就绪的Navion电磁导航系统演示了磁驱动倒立摆的摆起控制，解决了远平衡状态下的轨迹跟踪问题。方法结合轨迹优化、LQR与迭代学习控制，ILC在六次迭代内成功实现摆起，且学习到的校正与高保真模型预测一致，为应对电磁驱动的不确定性提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁导航系统在微创手术和靶向药物递送中备受关注，但现有研究多依赖准静态控制。近期动态方法虽展示优势，远平衡状态下的轨迹跟踪仍缺乏探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，并通过倒立摆摆起控制验证方法普适性，为磁驱动设备（如导管、导丝）提供新控制策略。

Method: 采用Navion电磁导航系统，结合考虑系统内部动力学的轨迹优化、时变线性二次调节器（LQR）状态反馈以及迭代学习控制（ILC）。ILC利用历史试验数据与系统动力学模型，逐步优化前馈指令，克服了LQR单独使用时因磁驱动复杂现象导致的失败。

Result: ILC在六次迭代内成功实现磁驱动倒立摆的摆起控制。实验后分析表明，学习得到的ILC校正项与高保真磁场模型校准预测的扭矩误差高度吻合，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究证明，结合LQR与ILC的动态控制策略可有效解决电磁导航系统在远平衡状态下的轨迹跟踪问题。ILC展现出的自适应学习能力，为应对电磁驱动中的不确定性（如患者生理运动、场模型校准误差）提供了有前景的解决方案，并可推广至其他磁驱动医疗器械的控制。

Abstract: Electromagnetic Navigation Systems (eMNS) have gained considerable attention for minimally invasive surgery and targeted drug delivery. While most of the literature relies on quasi-static control of these systems, recent work has demonstrated the benefits of dynamic approaches. However, trajectory tracking far from equilibrium states remains largely unaddressed. We close this gap by demonstrating the first swing-up of a magnetically actuated inverted pendulum using the clinically-ready Navion eMNS. Although the inverted pendulum is not clinically relevant in itself, the proposed method utilizes torques and forces as control objectives, making it applicable to other magnetically actuated devices such as catheters and guidewires. Our approach combines trajectory optimization that accounts for internal eMNS dynamics with time-varying Linear Quadratic Regulator (LQR) state feedback and Iterative Learning Control (ILC), which leverages previous trial data and the system's dynamic model to progressively refine the feedforward command. While LQR alone fails due to the complex phenomena of magnetic actuation, ILC enables successful swing-up within six iterations. Furthermore, post-experimental analysis reveals that the learned ILC correction closely matches the torque discrepancy predicted by high-fidelity magnetic field model calibration, suggesting learning and adaptation as a promising tool to deal with uncertainties in electromagnetic actuation arising, e.g., from patient-specific physiological motion patterns and field model calibration inaccuracies.

</details>


### [83] [An Attention-based Model for Robust Forecasting with Missing Modality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13970)
*Zhitian Zhang,Wenjie Zi,Yunduz Rakhmangulova,Saghar Irandoust,Hossein Hajimirsadeghi,Thibaut Durand*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件变分自编码器（CVAE）和Transformer架构的多模态模型，能够在训练和推理阶段同时处理缺失模态的问题，通过注意力机制学习统一的固定维度表示，并在多个机器人学习任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态机器人学习中，真实场景下的传感器数据常不完整，而现有方法通常假设所有模态均可用，这限制了其在机器人感知与决策中的应用。因此，需要一种能处理缺失模态的多模态学习框架。

Method: 模型采用条件变分自编码器（CVAE）框架，结合基于Transformer的架构，利用注意力机制融合多模态数据，即使部分模态缺失，也能学习到统一且固定维度的表示。模型可在训练和推理时处理缺失模态。

Result: 在五个公开的多模态数据集上，针对人类轨迹预测和机器人操纵预测两项任务进行评估。实验表明，该模型能有效从不完整数据中学习，且在性能上优于现有的多模态融合方法。

Conclusion: 提出的多模态模型成功解决了训练与推理阶段缺失模态的挑战，通过注意力机制实现了鲁棒的多模态表示学习，在机器人学习任务中展现出优越性能，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Learning with missing modalities is a fundamental challenge in multimodal robot learning, as real-world robotic systems often operate in environments with incomplete sensor data. Attention-based models are appealing for processing multimodal data because they can handle multiple modalities with a single backbone network. However, most multimodal models assume that all modalities are available during both training and inference, limiting their applicability in robotic perception and decision-making. In this paper, we introduce a multimodal model designed to handle missing modalities during both training and inference. The model is formulated as a conditional variational autoencoder (CVAE) and incorporates a transformer-based architecture that leverages attention mechanisms to learn a unified, fixed-dimensional representation, even when some modalities are missing. We show that our proposed model can be trained with missing modalities while approximating a robust representation of all modalities. We evaluate our approach on five multimodal datasets across two robot learning tasks: human trajectory prediction and robot manipulation forecasting. Experimental results demonstrate that our model effectively learns from incomplete data and is superior to prior multimodal fusion approaches.

</details>


### [84] [SplatlessDF: Continuous Distance Field Mapping with Non-Splatting Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13990)
*Monisha Mushtary Uttsha,Lan Wu,Teresa Vidal-Calleja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SplatlessDF，一种基于高斯splatting思想、从空间视角使用各向异性高斯元素构建连续距离场（DF）的框架，用于机器人导航的距离与梯度查询，并能与2D高斯splatting（2DGS）结合实现统一的DF与渲染学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高斯splatting（GS）方法虽能高效表示场景以进行高质量重建与渲染，但主要从光度视角出发，缺乏对机器人任务（如导航）所需的空间距离场的有效支持。因此，本文旨在将GS原理拓展至空间距离场建模，以服务于机器人导航等下游任务。

Method: 提出SplatlessDF框架：1）从空间视角直接参数化各向异性高斯元素，通过优化直接学习可微的距离场（DF），支持在空间域查询距离与梯度；2）提供两种配置：a) 仅距离场的独立DF公式化；b) 将DF与2D高斯splatting（2DGS）耦合的联合公式化，从而形成一个基于高斯原语、能同时学习连续DF、表面模型并支持光度渲染的统一框架。

Result: 实验表明：1）独立的DF公式化能实现高效且精确的距离与梯度查询；2）与2DGS结合的联合公式化在改善渲染几何质量的同时，也能成功建模连续的距离场。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，高斯splatting风格的表示不仅适用于表面建模与渲染，还能为机器人导航构建有效的映射表示，展现出其作为统一框架在三维感知与机器人任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Recent Gaussian splatting (GS) methods have shown that scenes can be represented efficiently with optimisable Gaussians for high-quality reconstruction and rendering. In this paper, building on this principle, we introduce SplatlessDF, a continuous distance field (DF) mapping framework that uses anisotropic Gaussian elements from a spatial rather than photometric perspective. SplatlessDF directly parameterises the Gaussians and optimises to recover a differentiable DF, enabling distances and gradients to be queried in the spatial domain for downstream robotic tasks such as navigation. Furthermore, SplatlessDF can be coupled with 2D Gaussian splatting (2DGS), providing a unified framework based solely on Gaussian primitives that can learn continuous DF and surface models and supports photometric rendering. We consider two settings: a standalone DF-only formulation and a joint DF-rendering formulation coupled with 2DGS. Experiments show that the standalone formulation provides efficient and accurate distance and gradient queries, while the joint formulation improves rendering geometry and simultaneously models a continuous DF. These results highlight the potential of GS-style representations not only for surface modelling and rendering but also for mapping representations suited to robotic navigation.

</details>


### [85] [From Attacks to Curricula: Learnability-Guided Adversarial Training for Safe Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14032)
*Yuewen Mei,Tong Nie,Jie Sun,Haotian Shi,Wei Ma,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了AlignADV，一种学习能力引导的闭环对抗训练框架，将对抗性场景转化为可解决且与能力对齐的课程，从而提升自动驾驶策略的训练效率和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶对抗训练流程存在两个核心问题：1）以攻击为导向的场景生成器往往合成无法解决的极端情境，这会损害策略学习；2）启发式采样器忽略了驾驶策略不断演变的能力，导致样本效率低下和收敛延迟。

Method: AlignADV框架包含两个关键创新：1）将对抗性场景生成重新定义为偏好对齐问题，并采用直接偏好优化来引导生成器产生关键但可解决的场景；2）引入行为指纹来捕捉演变中策略的内在特征，并构建一个多模态能力预测模型，无需昂贵的闭环模拟即可预测策略性能。结合这两者，设计了动态课程采样机制，优先针对当前策略弱点的场景进行训练。

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion数据集上的实验表明，AlignADV显著提升了收敛效率和最终性能。与基线方法相比，训练步骤最多可减少40.6%，同时在正常和对抗性交通条件下均降低了碰撞率并提高了路线完成率。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，自动驾驶训练正从“攻击导向的场景生成”转向“学习能力引导的策略改进”。AlignADV框架为更安全、更高效的自动驾驶训练提供了一个有原则、系统性的方向。

Abstract: Closed-loop adversarial training improves autonomous driving safety by exposing policies to rare safety-critical scenarios. Standard pipelines first generate adversarial scenarios and then sample them for policy optimization. However, most existing frameworks remain attack-oriented: collision-driven generators often synthesize unsolvable extreme situations, which can degrade learning, while heuristic samplers ignore the evolving capability of the driving policy, causing sample inefficiency and delayed convergence. We propose AlignADV, a learnability-guided closed-loop adversarial training framework that converts adversarial scenarios into resolvable and capability-aligned curricula. First, we reformulate adversarial scenario generation as a preference alignment problem and employ direct preference optimization to guide the generator toward critical yet resolvable scenarios. Second, we introduce behavioral fingerprints to capture the intrinsic characteristics of the evolving policy and construct a multi-modal capability prediction model that estimates policy performance without expensive closed-loop simulations. By combining resolvability-aligned scenarios with capability predictions, AlignADV develops a dynamic curriculum sampling mechanism that prioritizes scenarios targeting the current policy's vulnerabilities. Experiments on the Waymo Open Motion Dataset demonstrate that AlignADV improves convergence efficiency and final performance, reducing training steps by up to 40.6 percent compared with baseline methods while lowering collision rate and improving route completion under both normal and adversarial traffic conditions. These results highlight a shift from attack-oriented scenario generation to learnability-guided policy improvement, offering a principled direction for safer and more efficient autonomous driving training. Project page: https://meiyuewen.github.io/AlignADV/.

</details>


### [86] [ReactSim-Bench: Benchmarking Reactive Behavior World Model Simulation in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14058)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Yanlun Peng,Jianing Zhang,Xianda Guo,Zehan Huang,Haoran Liu,Qifeng Li,Shaofeng Zhang,Xiaosong Jia,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了ReactSim-Bench，一个专门用于评估自动驾驶中行为世界模型模拟器反应能力的基准。它通过解耦智能体和自动驾驶车辆(AV)的控制，使用不同于日志的AV行为作为输入，并建立了安全与规则合规性的评估体系，对多种架构的最新模型进行了系统评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的行为模拟基准通常不直接测量“反应能力”这一关键特性。它们往往让模拟器同时控制AV和周围智能体，并通过日志相似性或开环预测指标来评估真实性，这无法有效评估模拟器对非日志AV行为做出真实反应的能力。

Method: 1. 提出ReactSim-Bench基准，核心是解耦控制：将AV的控制独立出来，使用不同于原始日志的行为作为输入，迫使周围智能体做出反应。
2. 构建数据生成流程：利用AV规划模型生成候选的、不同于日志的AV行为，并通过规则和人工验证进行过滤。
3. 设计评估指标：使用碰撞指标、基于地图的指标和运动可行性指标来评估反应行为的安全性与规则遵守情况。

Result: 1. 构建了包含三个类别、共计2,636个测试场景的数据集。
2. 对多种架构（基于Transformer、基于扩散、基于下一令牌预测）的先进模型进行了系统性的评估。
3. 分析了重新规划频率对模型性能的影响，并提供了相关见解。

Conclusion: ReactSim-Bench为评估自动驾驶世界模型的反应能力提供了首个专门且系统化的基准。研究表明，不同架构的模型在反应能力上存在差异，且重新规划频率是影响性能的关键因素。该工作为未来提升模拟器反应能力的研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Reactive capability is a key property of data-driven behavior world model simulators for autonomous driving simulation systems. With this capability, simulated world agents can respond feasibly to autonomous vehicle (AV) behaviors that differ from the log. However, existing behavior simulation benchmarks do not directly measure reactive capability. They often let the simulator jointly control the AV and surrounding agents and evaluate realism through log similarity or open-loop prediction metrics. In this work, we introduce ReactSim-Bench for evaluating the reactive capability of behavior world model simulation in autonomous driving. We decouple the control of agents and the AV, using AV behaviors that differ from the log and require agents to respond as independent AV inputs. To obtain these AV behaviors, we construct a pipeline that uses an AV planner model to generate candidate behaviors and filters the data using rules and manual verification. Collision metrics, map-based metrics, and kinematic feasibility metrics are used to evaluate the safety and rule compliance of reactive responses. We construct 2,636 test scenarios with three categories and conduct a systematic evaluation of state-of-the-art models across multiple architectures, including Transformer-based, diffusion-based, and next-token-prediction-based models. We further analyze how replan frequency affects performance and provide insights for future studies.

</details>


### [87] [Semidefinite Relaxations for Collision-Free Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14063)
*Bernhard Paus Graesdal,Alexandre Amice,Pablo A. Parrilo,Russ Tedrake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次对无碰撞运动规划中的半定松弛方法进行了理论分析。研究聚焦于一个概念简单但能捕捉规划难点的场景：点机器人在n维空间中穿越球形障碍物。作者将该问题精确表述为多项式曲线上的非凸问题，并提出一种半定松弛方法。关键理论贡献包括：证明求解该凸松弛等价于在更高维空间全局最优求解相关问题，并揭示了松弛紧致的充要条件；发现存在对称性简化，使半正定锥的大小随多项式次数线性增长，与环境维度无关。实验表明，该方法比直接非线性规划转录法快10-100倍，求解时间方差小，能可靠找到局部最优路径，并作为凸转向函数在RRT规划器中成功应用于最小snap四旋翼轨迹规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于无碰撞运动规划问题的计算挑战。传统非凸优化求解困难，而半定松弛作为凸优化方法有望提供高效且理论可靠的近似。本文旨在首次对该领域半定松弛进行理论分析，填补空白，并为实际规划算法提供坚实的数学基础与高效工具。

Method: 1. 问题建模：将点机器人在球形障碍物环境中的路径规划问题，建模为具有连续性约束和平方导数代价、定义在多项式曲线上的精确非凸优化问题。
2. 松弛方法：提出一种自然的半定松弛（SDP relaxation）来近似求解该非凸问题。
3. 理论分析：
   a. 几何解释：证明求解所得凸松弛等价于在可能更高维度的空间中全局最优求解一个相关的运动规划问题。
   b. 紧致条件：基于上述几何解释，推导出松弛为“紧”（即得到原始问题最优解）的充要条件，并阐明松弛“松”的直观原因。
   c. 对称性简化：发现并利用问题中的对称性，使得最终的半正定松弛问题规模显著减小。其半正定锥的大小仅随所用多项式次数线性增长，与机器人运动的环境维度 n 无关。

Result: 1. 理论贡献：首次为无碰撞运动规划的半定松弛提供了理论分析框架，包括紧致性条件和问题规模缩减的理论依据。
2. 计算效率：数值实验表明，该半定松弛方法比使用SNOPT和IPOPT直接求解非线性规划转录问题快10到100倍，并且求解时间的方差显著更低。
3. 解决方案质量：该方法能够可靠地找到原始非凸问题的局部最优路径。
4. 应用验证：成功将该松弛方法用作RRT（快速扩展随机树）规划器中的凸转向函数，实现了具有C4连续性的最小snap四旋翼飞行器轨迹规划。

Conclusion: 本文证明，针对无碰撞运动规划这一典型非凸问题，精心设计的半定松弛不仅具有坚实的理论基础（与特定高维空间的全局最优解等价），而且通过利用对称性可以实现计算规模的极大缩减。该方法在效率和稳定性上远超传统的直接非线性优化求解器，并能为运动规划算法（如RRT）提供高效的子程序，成功应用于高阶连续的四旋翼轨迹规划。这项工作为运动规划中的凸优化方法开辟了新的理论视角和高效工具。

Abstract: We study semidefinite relaxations for collision-free motion planning. We focus on a point robot moving from start to goal through spherical obstacles in $\mathbb{R}^n$, subject to path continuity constraints and squared derivative costs; a setting that is conceptually simple yet captures the hardness of collision-free motion planning. We formulate this problem exactly as a nonconvex problem over polynomial curves, and present a natural semidefinite relaxation. We contribute two key theoretical insights; to our knowledge this is the first theoretical analysis of semidefinite relaxations for collision-free motion planning. First, we show that solving the convex relaxation is equivalent to solving, to global optimality, a related motion planning problem in a potentially higher-dimensional space. This geometric interpretation yields necessary and sufficient conditions for tightness, and a clear intuition for when the relaxation is loose. Second, we show that the relaxation admits a symmetry reduction that makes it significantly smaller than one might expect, with positive semidefinite cone sizes that scale linearly with the polynomial degree and are independent of the ambient dimension. The resulting relaxation is 10 to 100 times faster than direct nonlinear programming transcriptions solved with SNOPT and IPOPT, exhibits significantly lower variance in solve times, and reliably finds a locally optimal path for the original problem. We demonstrate its effectiveness as a convex steering function in an RRT planner for minimum-snap quadrotor planning with $C^4$ continuous trajectories.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进现有下水道管道检查机器人的性能，解决其牵引力不足和跨越障碍能力差的问题。

Method: 通过连接推进单元提升牵引力；采用差分机构通过单根线绳实现姿态调整以适应管径变化；设计基于驱动轮电机电流负载检测障碍物接触并放松线绳的控制策略。

Result: 模拟管道实验验证了所提出的控制方法，电流波形负载比较显示该方法显著提高了铰接机器人的跨越能力。

Abstract: This paper proposes, an improved version of the 3 in sewer pipe inspection robot equipped with an emergency evacuation mechanism. The low traction force and poor stepover capability, which were challenges of the first version, have been improved by simply connecting the propulsion units. The coupled propulsion units feature a differential mechanism capable of posture changes via a single wire, enabling adaptation to pipe diameter variations. To traverse obstacles like pipe joints, a control method was devised that detects obstacle contact through current load on the drive wheel motors and slackens the wire. This method was verified through simulated pipe experiments. Load comparisons were made using current waveforms applied to the drive wheels. Our proposed control method significantly improved the step-over capability of the new articulated robots.

</details>
